50 #include <stdint.h>

52 /*********************************************************************/
53 extern char __MBAR[];
54 
55 /*********************************************************************
56 *
57 * Cache Control Register (CACR)
58 *
59 *********************************************************************/
60 
61 /* Bit definitions and macros for MCF548X_CACR */
62 #define MCF548X_CACR_DEC (0x80000000)
63 #define MCF548X_CACR_DW (0x40000000)
64 #define MCF548X_CACR_DESB (0x20000000)
65 #define MCF548X_CACR_DDPI (0x10000000)
66 #define MCF548X_CACR_DHLCK (0x08000000)
67 #define MCF548X_CACR_DDCM(x) (((x)<<25)&0x06000000)
68 #define MCF548X_CACR_DCINVA (0x01000000)
69 #define MCF548X_CACR_DDSP (0x00800000)
70 #define MCF548X_CACR_BEC (0x00080000)
71 #define MCF548X_CACR_BCINVA (0x00040000)
72 #define MCF548X_CACR_IEC (0x00008000)
73 #define MCF548X_CACR_DNFB (0x00002000)
74 #define MCF548X_CACR_IDPI (0x00001000)
75 #define MCF548X_CACR_IHLCK (0x00000800)
76 #define MCF548X_CACR_IDCM (0x00000400)
77 #define MCF548X_CACR_ICINVA (0x00000100)
78 #define MCF548X_CACR_IDSP (0x00000080)
79 #define MCF548X_CACR_EUSP (0x00000020)
80 #define MCF548X_CACR_DF (0x00000010)
81 
82 /* Bit definitions and macros for MCF548X_CACR_DDCM (data cache mode) */
83 #define DCACHE_ON_WRIGHTTHROUGH 0
84 #define DCACHE_ON_COPYBACK 1
85 #define DCACHE_OFF_PRECISE 2
86 #define DCACHE_OFF_IMPRECISE 3
87 
88 /*********************************************************************
89 *
90 * Access Control Registers (ACR0-3)
91 *
92 *********************************************************************/
93 
94 /* Bit definitions and macros for MCF548X_ACRn */
95 #define MCF548X_ACR_BA(x) ((x)&0xFF000000)
96 #define MCF548X_ACR_ADMSK_AMM(x) (((x)>=0x1000000) ? (((x)&0xFF000000)>>8) : (((x)&0x00FF0000)|0x00000400))
97 #define MCF548X_ACR_E (0x00008000)
98 #define MCF548X_ACR_S(x) (((x)<<13)&0x00006000)
99 #define MCF548X_ACR_CM(x) (((x)<<5)&0x00000060)
100 #define MCF548X_ACR_SP (0x00000008)
101 #define MCF548X_ACR_W (0x00000004)
102 
103 /* Bit definitions and macros for MCF548X_ACR_S (supervisor/user access) */
104 #define S_ACCESS_USER 0
105 #define S_ACCESS_SUPV 1
106 #define S_ACCESS_BOTH 2
107 
108 /* Bit definitions and macros for MCF548X_ACR_CM (cache mode) */
109 #define CM_ON_WRIGHTTHROUGH 0
110 #define CM_ON_COPYBACK 1
111 #define CM_OFF_PRECISE 2
112 #define CM_OFF_IMPRECISE 3
113

115 *
116 * System PLL Control Register (SPCR)
117 *
118 *********************************************************************/
119 
120 /* Register read/write macro */
121 #define MCF548X_PLL_SPCR (*(volatile uint32_t*)((uintptr_t)__MBAR + (0x000300)))
122 
123 /* Bit definitions and macros for MCF548X_PLL_SPCR (supervisor/user access) */
124 #define MCF548X_PLL_SPCR_PLLK 0x80000000
125 #define MCF548X_PLL_SPCR_COREN 0x00004000
126 #define MCF548X_PLL_SPCR_CRYENB 0x00002000

128 #define MCF548X_PLL_SPCR_CAN1EN 0x00000800
129 #define MCF548X_PLL_SPCR_PSCEN 0x00000200
130 #define MCF548X_PLL_SPCR_USBEN 0x00000080
131 #define MCF548X_PLL_SPCR_FEC1EN 0x00000040
132 #define MCF548X_PLL_SPCR_FEC0EN 0x00000020
133 #define MCF548X_PLL_SPCR_DMAEN 0x00000010
134 #define MCF548X_PLL_SPCR_CAN0EN 0x00000008
135 #define MCF548X_PLL_SPCR_FBEN 0x00000004
136 #define MCF548X_PLL_SPCR_PCIEN 0x00000002
137 #define MCF548X_PLL_SPCR_MEMEN 0x00000001
